| P119 | 12 Nov 2026 | **J9-a, J9-b, J9-c: the flip of the algebra leaves the ordinary numbers alone and reverses every other direction; closure is asked of every reversed direction; no table is assumed; read-backs R7 and R8-(i) confirmed (PI).** The session read the list of the M1 handover, showed the lines of canon M1 v6.3 that J9 turns on in full (ll.187, 193, 209, 449, 453, 455, 458, 461, 465, 467, 469, 471, 489, 546–552; for Definition 2.7, ll.211, 214, 217, 536, 566, 611, 693, 837, 871), put nine numbered assumptions (among them the division of J9 into four parts, the architect's: a, which flip Lemma 5.1 means; b, "values in the fixed set of *" against the inverse a*/N(a); c, "every corner subalgebra" before the corners are counted; d, the two levels of Definition 2.7) and four questions. **Question 1, as put:** "The paper flips the algebra: "exchanging growth and decay: composition run backwards". (ii) Only the special lines that later become the three complex corners, the algebra being taken from the start to have the 1, i, j, k pattern, with only the squares (−1 or +1) left to settle. (iii) Something else."
